## `AFG-6920-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Bank-Confirmation Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Consolidated Financial Statement Elimination & IFRS Compliance Quality Reviewer
